# Batch 45 — beat jpwpmedia: Japanese online media and magazines already in the registry — every WordPress collection their REST root advertises (posts, pages, authors, custom post types)
# id|mode|want|category|record_type|tags|portal|name|name_ja|url|probe|description|opts
#
# Hosts came from the registry itself: every .jp host already proven live by a
# different row in the tree (RSS, HTML, CSV …) was asked for its WordPress REST root
# (the Link rel="https://api.w.org/" its homepage advertises, else /wp-json), then
# for its post types (/wp/v2/types), then for one page of each collection. Only
# collections that answered 200 with >=1 record are here; every other answer is in
# docs/rejected-sources-batch45.tsv. Measured 2026-10-19.
#
